- [Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm.md): Run hardware-encrypted Virtual Machines on IONOS CLOUD using AMD SEV-SNP. Protect data in use from hypervisors, cloud operators, and administrators, with cryptographic proof.
- [Overview](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/overview.md): IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM: AMD SEV-SNP hardware-encrypted VMs for regulated workloads.
- [Features and Benefits](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/overview/features-and-benefits.md): IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM features and benefits: hardware encryption, attestation, disk encryption, and sovereignty.
- [Security and Trust Model](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/overview/security-and-trust-model.md): Security model includes hardware-enforced operator exclusion, startup integrity, and key management
- [Limitations](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/overview/limitations.md): IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM limitations: hardware constraints, no live migration, API access, and key operational trade-offs.
- [Use Cases](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/use-cases.md): IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM use cases for regulated industries and zero-trust workloads.
- [How-Tos](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/how-tos.md): Confidential VM DCD how-to guides. Create and manage Confidential VMs in the Data Center Designer.
- [Set Up a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/how-tos/set-up-confidential-vm-dcd.md): Create a Confidential VM in the DCD using an uploaded CC image with hardware-enforced memory encryption.
- [Start a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/how-tos/start-confidential-vm-dcd.md): Start a stopped Confidential VM using the IONOS CLOUD DCD. Starting the VM runs the full hardware-enforced boot sequence and generates a new memory encryption key.
- [Stop a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/how-tos/stop-confidential-vm-dcd.md): Stop a Confidential VM in the IONOS CLOUD DCD to deallocate resources and suspend billing. OS-level shutdown is not sufficient.
- [Reset a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/how-tos/reset-confidential-vm-dcd.md): Reset a Confidential VM in IONOS CLOUD. The full AMD SEV-SNP hardware-enforced boot sequence runs again on reset—and if attestation is configured, its validation flow runs as well.
- [Delete a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/how-tos/delete-confidential-vm-dcd.md): Delete a Confidential VM in the IONOS CLOUD DCD. Remove the VM and its associated storage, deallocate IP addresses, and stop billing immediately.

- [API How-Tos](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/api-how-tos.md): IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM how-to guides for provisioning, image preparation, and VM creation.
- [Prepare a Confidential VM Image](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/api-how-tos/prepare-confidential-vm-image.md): Build a Confidential VM image with LUKS encryption, LAUNCH\_ARTIFACTS partition, firmware, and attestation support for IONOS CLOUD.
- [Create a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/api-how-tos/create-confidential-vm.md): Create an IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM using the Cloud API with AMD SEV-SNP hardware enforcement.
- [Attest a Confidential VM](https://docs.ionos.com/cloud/compute-services/compute-engine/confidential-computing-vm/api-how-tos/attest-confidential-vm.md): Cryptographically verify that your IONOS CLOUD Confidential VM runs an unmodified software stack on genuine AMD SEV-SNP hardware.
